{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"bluffs","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"That is only bluff, or That is only a bluff.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An act of bluffing; a false expression of the strength of one’s position in order to intimidate or deceive; braggadocio."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"John’s bet was a bluff: he bet without even so much as a pair.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An attempt to represent oneself as holding a stronger hand than one actually does."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["card-games","poker"]},{"glosses":["The card game poker."],"tags":["US","dated","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["One who bluffs; a bluffer."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["Pretense, excuse."],"tags":["countable","dated","slang","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/blʌf/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/blɐf/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/blʊf/","tags":["Northern-England"]}],"upstream_line":235196,"upstream_offset":1152236289,"upstream_record_sha256":"d9291828e3a49f8e692ba79f3ef1ddb1f7c4bb13aa2a3d3c0f9fedac4f88b46a","word":"bluff"}

{"forms":[{"form":"fermions","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The fermions treated by the Standard Model are the (composite) baryons and the (elementary) leptons and quarks.","type":"example"},{"text":"According to the spin–statistics theorem, the wavefunction of a system of identical fermions (particles of half-integer spin) is antisymmetric under the operation of swapping any two particles.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any elementary or composite particle that has half-integer spin and thus obeys Fermi–Dirac statistics and the Pauli exclusion principle (equivalently, a particle for which the wavefunction of any system of identical such particles changes sign whenever two are swapped); a baryon, a lepton or a quark;"],"qualifier":"Standard Model","tags":["particle"],"topics":["natural-sciences","physical-sciences","physics"]},{"glosses":["Any elementary or composite particle that has half-integer spin and thus obeys Fermi–Dirac statistics and the Pauli exclusion principle (equivalently, a particle for which the wavefunction of any system of identical such particles changes sign whenever two are swapped); a baryon, a lepton or a quark;\n(slightly more loosely) any such particle or any composite particle composed of fermions.","any such particle or any composite particle composed of fermions."],"qualifier":"Standard Model; slightly more loosely","tags":["particle"],"topics":["natural-sciences","physical-sciences","physics"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈfɜːmɪɒn/"}],"synonyms":[{"word":"fermion"}],"upstream_line":248591,"upstream_offset":1233569781,"upstream_record_sha256":"8b26c6ce81e6d4e8534a6893ebe07946f7981352d2a8d9780658478fad6f678d","word":"fermion"}

{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"rattles","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"rattling","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"rattled","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"rattled","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"to rattle a chain","type":"example"},{"text":"Rattle the can of cat treats if you need to find Fluffy.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To create a rattling sound by shaking or striking."],"tags":["ergative","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To scare, startle, unsettle, or unnerve."],"tags":["figuratively","informal","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I wish the dashboard in my car would quit rattling.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To make a rattling noise; to make noise by or from shaking."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To assail, annoy, or stun with a rattling noise."],"tags":["obsolete","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To scold; to rail at."],"tags":["obsolete","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"We rattled along for a couple of miles.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To drive or ride briskly, so as to make a clattering."]},{"examples":[{"text":"She rattled on for an hour.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To make a clatter with one's voice; to talk rapidly and idly; often with on or away."]},{"glosses":["To experience withdrawal from drugs."],"tags":["UK","slang"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɹæ.təl/"},{"ipa":"[ˈɹæ.tɫ̩]"}],"upstream_line":288811,"upstream_offset":1441111748,"upstream_record_sha256":"a2338112c67d6203bea5a6a8b8ae3c073acfb42bd85cfb3f2149e251a495c176","word":"rattle"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"rattles","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Object that rattles.","Any of various plants of the genera Rhinanthus and Pedicularis, whose seeds produce a rattling noise in the wind."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Object that rattles.","A baby’s toy designed to make sound when shaken, usually containing loose grains or pellets in a hollow container."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Object that rattles.","A musical instrument that makes a rattling sound."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]},{"glosses":["Object that rattles.","A device which produces a loud rattling sound, especially one having a ratchet mechanism and spun round on a handle."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The rattle of the rattlesnake is composed of the hardened terminal scales, loosened in succession, but not cast off, and modified in form so as to make a series of loose, hollow joints.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Object that rattles.","The set of rings at the end of a rattlesnake's tail which produce a rattling sound."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences","zoology"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I wish they would fix the rattle under my dashboard.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Rattling sound.","A rapid succession of percussive sounds, as made by loose objects shaking or vibrating against one another."],"tags":["countable","onomatopoeic","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Rattling sound.","Noisy, rapid talk; croup; a death rattle."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɹæ.təl/"},{"ipa":"[ˈɹæ.tɫ̩]"}],"upstream_line":288812,"upstream_offset":1441139455,"upstream_record_sha256":"4efb1ed9f6785209fdbf9a77d3e9b2973b14811cc0d37d16ff7f8fd2d34326cc","word":"rattle"}
